      </section>
    
  </div>

  <script>
    function switchListTab(id, tabType) {
      const tabNewBtn = document.getElementById('tab-btn-new-' + id);
      const tabUpdatedBtn = document.getElementById('tab-btn-updated-' + id);
      const tabNewContent = document.getElementById('tab-content-new-' + id);
      const tabUpdatedContent = document.getElementById('tab-content-updated-' + id);

      if (tabType === 'new') {
        
        tabNewBtn.classList.remove('opacity-40', 'hover:opacity-80');
        tabNewBtn.classList.add('opacity-100');
        tabUpdatedBtn.classList.remove('opacity-100');
        tabUpdatedBtn.classList.add('opacity-40', 'hover:opacity-80');

        
        tabUpdatedContent.classList.add('hidden');
        tabUpdatedContent.classList.remove('opacity-100');
        tabUpdatedContent.classList.add('opacity-0');

        tabNewContent.classList.remove('hidden');
        setTimeout(() => {
          tabNewContent.classList.remove('opacity-0');
          tabNewContent.classList.add('opacity-100');
        }, 50);
      } else {
        
        tabUpdatedBtn.classList.remove('opacity-40', 'hover:opacity-80');
        tabUpdatedBtn.classList.add('opacity-100');
        tabNewBtn.classList.remove('opacity-100');
        tabNewBtn.classList.add('opacity-40', 'hover:opacity-80');

        
        tabNewContent.classList.add('hidden');
        tabNewContent.classList.remove('opacity-100');
        tabNewContent.classList.add('opacity-0');

        tabUpdatedContent.classList.remove('hidden');
        setTimeout(() => {
          tabUpdatedContent.classList.remove('opacity-0');
          tabUpdatedContent.classList.add('opacity-100');
        }, 50);
      }
    }

    
    (function() {
      document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        if (urlParams.get('tab') === 'updated') {
          switchListTab('list-недавние', 'updated');
        }
      });
    })();
  </script>
